# M0 gate summary

- **For:** the product owner, ahead of the M0 exit review (plan Sections 21.1, 21.2, 22.4 and 23.3).
- **State:** agent-written. It records your decisions of 22 October 2026, all 128 items of the M0 decision page (Sections 1, 3 and 4), and your answers of 23 October 2026 to the five points those decisions left open (Section 1, item 4; as relayed, `evidence/M0-Decisions/followup/owner-answers-2026-10-23.md`), and those are the only things in it you have signed or decided: no sign-off of a plan 23.3 role and no device result is recorded yet. Its inputs (reports, ADRs, contracts, runbook, amendments, logs) were agent-reviewed as merged; the agent edits since 19 October 2026 (the M0 final fix, the re-pin round and its fix, the records of your decisions and their assembly, the fixes of their reviews, the follow-up and its four fixes, the record of item 4's drop-in run and its two fixes, and this summary's own updates) have had no accepting review: each review found more to fix, and the last two fixes, the follow-up's fourth and that record's second, have not been reviewed, nor has the M0 exit preparation of 24 October 2026 (Sections 6.2 and 8). Human sign-off pending on everything below except your decisions. (This line's history: Section 6.2, and Section 6.3, item 88.)
- **Evidence paths** are relative to `Docs/Stage0/` unless they start with a repository folder. **Dates.** A run or an event (a CI run, Xcode's update) is dated by UTC, with its time where known, and with the build Mac's date beside it wherever that differs: the build Mac's clock (NZDT, UTC+13) reads the next day from 11:00 UTC. The rounds of work and the dated notes of the documents ("until 20 October 2026 this line said ...") carry the build Mac's date, as their commits do: the fix's second round worked on 19 October 2026 UTC, which its notes date 20 October.
- **Versions, branch, sources and this summary's own checks:** Section 8 (moved there from this header on 20 October 2026: M0 final review round 3, R7-09).

## 1. In short

**Recommendation** (Section 7): M0 cannot be approved yet. No item of the M0 decision page is open: on 23 October 2026 you answered the five points your decisions had left open (item 4): H-1's exception also covers pull request #5 and the merge of `m0-decisions` into `main` before the exit, and no other merge, the branch rule staying and M0 staying pending; a damaged sources file or import ledger (PO-12) is set aside and recovered; PO-41 to PO-44 are yours for now, their recommended defaults applying to your own and TestFlight builds with the notices the licenses require, and a lawyer's review comes before any public App Store release (PO-42 had no recommended option, so its own question stays open for that review: item 4); you have an iPad with a keyboard and a trackpad or mouse for Session 9 (whether it runs the iPadOS 26 that Session 9 needs is checked before Session 0: item 1); and you confirmed the three readings of your decisions (D-6, D-7 and D-9 (b)). Decisions are still yours beyond the page, among them, in Session 10: as technical lead, the G0.2 report's open questions 5-7 and 12, G0.1 EPUB's open question 4 (who reports four Readium findings upstream) and G0.4's question for M2; with the design review, whether to accept the large content viewer's two stated limits (as the accessibility reviewer) and the two decisions G0.5 AI step 9.2 asks for with its wording review (runbook Section 6); and, as product owner, after runs 8 and 9, the outcome of D03, whether quick capture can be advertised (item 3). Before the exit, only if you want it: whether H-1's exception also covers bringing agent work done during the sessions, or your session records, to `main` (item 1). And where these records read your answers or your instruction, they ask you to correct the reading if it is wrong: that your answer 3 does not name the companion's notarized builds (item 4), and that the roles your instruction does not name are yours (below). On 22 October 2026 you decided all 128 items of the M0 decision page: the amendments of plan drafts 0.5 and 0.7, decisions D01-D17, your 44 questions, the runbook's D-1 to D-10 and H-1. 123 follow the recommendation as the documents state it, and five do not (below). - **Before Session 10: ADR-0014's R4-01 and R4-02 closed.** They are two routes around the CI guard that keeps marmelroy/Zip 2.1.2 (CVE-2023-39135) out of product code (Section 6.1). ## 2. Criteria and deliverables

One row per sentence of plan 21.1 (split where a gate report gives a sentence several rows), per M0 deliverable of plan 21.2, and per ADR that plan 22.4 requires. **Status** is copied from the gate report row in the last column (for M0 deliverables and ADRs, from the record named there). Status values: passed-host, passed-simulator, failed, partial, pending-device, pending-human. No row is passed on a device: no device step has run.

**Checked on 19 October 2026.** `evidence/M0-Integration/commands/gate-refresh-check.py` compares the status of every row that cites a gate report with the status cell of each report row it cites, and the status of every ADR and deliverable row with the ADR's status line or the CI log it cites; every one agrees (`evidence/M0-Integration/gate-refresh-checks.log`, part 4), so no gate report status was changed. The M0 final fix ran the same check again on its last commit, with row 75 now read against the final complete run (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix/gate-final-checks.log`, part 3; this line cited part 4 until 20 October 2026, final review round 2 R2-08). On copies of the documents with three wrong statuses planted, it reports exactly the rows that rest on them (part 3b), so the comparison is not vacuous. The fix's second round ran both again on its last commit (`evidence/M0-Integration/final-fix-2/gate-final-checks.log`, parts 3 and 3b), and so did the toolchain re-pin round of 20 October 2026, with rows 66, 75, 78 and 82 read against ADR-0001's new status and the drop-in's run under the new pin (`evidence/M0-Integration/repin-27.1/gate-repin-checks.log`, parts 3 and 3b). The rows it cannot check mechanically (67-70 and 81: judgements on the plan's text and on the other rows) say what they rest on. Plan draft 0.6 changed no criterion of plan 21.1, the M0 row of 21.2 or the artifacts of 22.4, so the rows are those of the first version, with their numbers.

### 2.10 The CI from a clean tree, and since

**On 14 October 2026 UTC (15 October on the build Mac's clock), under Xcode 27.0**, `Docs/Stage0/evidence/M0-Integration/commands/ci-assembled.sh` ran `Scripts/ci.sh` twice on the assembled code, every drop-in on the simulator `Sylvia-M0-assemble` (iPhone 17, iOS 26.5), with `G02_LOOPBACK_ONLY=1` and every player at test volume 0.01, so every audio measurement in these runs was taken at test volume (integration log 7.6). Each run was to start from a clean tree (every `.build` and DerivedData deleted, then `Scripts/fixtures/fetch.sh` and the cache generators), but the wrapper logs no deletion, and the fixture check is logged for the first run only (`evidence/M0-Integration/prep-fixtures-assembled.log`), so for the second run both are unlogged observations (integration log 7.6). Both used Xcode 27.0 (27A266a), which the same runs' committed logs name (`evidence/M0-Integration/ci-full-clean-run-assembled-first.log` lines 58-59, `evidence/M0-Integration/ci-second-run-71-g05-recovery.log` lines 3-4), and `12-dependency-lock`'s toolchain check, which passes only with that build, passed in both. (Until 20 October 2026 this paragraph said both runs started from a deleted tree with the fixtures checked, and that their Xcode was inferred because their logs name none: final review round 2, R6-03 and evidence R2-04.)

## 3. Section 23.1 decisions

You approved D02-D15 as defaults on 6 October 2026 and amended D01 (plan 1.1). Draft 0.5 changed seven of them and added two, and on 22 October 2026 you approved all seventeen as this table states them (the M0 decision page). Several also need a technical, security, QA or design sign-off, which you give personally, each once the device sessions have produced its evidence (your instruction of 22 October 2026), and D17 the persistence owner's, a role the records assume is yours (Section 1); until then it stays pending. Section 1, item 3 says when each is given, and the runbook's Section 6 where each is recorded. "Confirmed" below means the host and simulator evidence supports the default as written, never that a device has. (Until 22 October 2026 the last column was "Who still decides", and named you and the other owners for each.)

**Your direction of 15 October 2026 (plan draft 0.6).** M3 keeps the selected passage and its source in view while a note is written and returns to the reading position; M5 adds Explain and Simplify question starters inside Ask, follow-ups in the same source-linked conversation and previews of a citation's retained evidence; a browser-based reader is a separate post-V1 workstream; native iOS/iPadOS V1 and the Mac companion stay first (plan 1.1, 4.1, 13.1, 21.2, 24; acceptance cases T55-T57). It is an approved product direction. It changes none of D01-D17, signs none of the changes above, and approves none of the pending amendments (`M0-plan-amendments.md` front matter); your decisions of 22 October 2026 did. Its new labels, such as Explain and Simplify, go through the same vocabulary process as the AI status phrases the amendments propose (A45, PO-28): design review, a fixture update and a snapshot re-baseline (plan 4.4.9; plan 13.1 says the same of the new labels and prompt templates). Gloss itself is a design reference, not a dependency: the plan records that the snapshot it was read at declares no reuse license, and ADR-0014's inventory now lists it so (`Docs/Licenses/third-party-inventory.md`, "Design references").

**The G0.6 interaction prototype predates that direction.** Its capture sheet shows the quotation once, at the top of a sheet that scrolls, with nothing that keeps the passage in view while the note is written (`Packages/Design/Sources/SylviaDesign/Components/CaptureSheet.swift`; `Apps/DesignCatalog/Sources/App/Layout.swift`, `SheetPage`); and on iPad its side pane is the Thoughts pane, while Ask opens as a page pushed in the reader (`Apps/DesignCatalog/Sources/Prototype/ReaderScreens.swift`, `Apps/DesignCatalog/Sources/Prototype/AskScreen.swift`), with no side-pane discussion. That is design input for M3 and M5, which build these interactions with T55-T57, not an M0 defect: row 72 stands. (Read from the committed sources; nothing was run for this.)

## 4. The questions only you could answer, and your decisions (22 October 2026)

**All decided on 22 October 2026.** On the M0 decision page you decided every question below: PO-13, PO-23, PO-33 and PO-39 of 4.1 and part (i) of D-1 in 4.3 otherwise than recommended, everything else as recommended. Each table's last column gives the decision; the options and recommended defaults stay as they were written, for the record. Three parts had no recommended default and stayed open: PO-12's sources file and import ledger, which your answers of 23 October 2026 decided (set aside and recovered), and PO-42, which stayed with counsel until that day, when you took it on for now with PO-41, PO-43 and PO-44, a lawyer's review coming before any public App Store release; PO-42's own question, which no default answers, stays open for that review (the last column gives both; Section 1, item 4). (Until the follow-up's second fix, 23 October 2026, this sentence had all three parts open only until your answers: the review of the follow-up, its finding HON4-05.) A decision is not a device result: where a decision waits on a device step or a sign-off, the column says so.

Taken from `M0-plan-amendments.md` Section 4 (options and recommended defaults as written there; read that table for the full reasoning) and the runbook. Nothing new is raised here, except decision D-10 (Section 4.3), which the M0 final review's second round asked for (R6-06). Where you choose another option, the plan text tagged in the amendments changes. Every question has a recommended default, except PO-12's sources file and import ledger and PO-42, which say why they have none, and every option says what it leads to: in a few words in the tables below, in full in the amendments' Section 4 and the runbook's Section 6. (Until 20 October 2026 this paragraph claimed that before several questions said it, and others said it for some of their options only: M0 final review round 3, R7-02; Section 6.3, item 16, names them. The re-pin round then made the amendments' table say it, but left this summary's short column naming only the options of 31 questions, and the amendments' PO-20, PO-32 and PO-42 (b) without their consequence, until the fix of that round's review the same day: R8-02, Section 6.3, item 20.) One question is read as decided: PO-35, the toolchain pin, on 20 October 2026, from your reply (its row).

**When each is decided** (as written before 22 October 2026, when you decided all of them, PO-33 and PO-39 included). Every question of 4.1 is for the M0 gate, because each fixes a Section 23.1 decision or plan text the M0 exit approves, except two the amendments leave unapplied in the plan as their milestones' choices: PO-33 (the bundled onboarding sample, M2) and PO-39 (playback control defaults, M4). Counsel's questions PO-41 to PO-44 are decided with ADR-0014 at the M0 gate as Section 7 lists them. The runbook's decisions of 4.3 come before the device sessions (D-1, D-2 and D-10; D-3, which is PO-35, is read as decided) or with the reviews of Session 10 (the others).

### 5.3 Git LFS: decided and implemented

- **Decision.** "Adopt Git LFS for the snapshot and evidence images once the current integration is complete. Use repository-local configuration, update CI and setup instructions, and verify a clean checkout. Leave existing history intact." (product owner, 13 October 2026).
- **Implemented on 19 October 2026** (integration log section 9; ADR-0002, "Binary test baselines and evidence images"). The adoption commit `adab8e2` (parent `504fac2`) adds `.gitattributes` and turns exactly the 777 matched images into LFS pointers: the 527 snapshot references (37,703,582 bytes) and the 250 Stage 0 evidence images (29,875,981 bytes), 67,579,563 bytes in all, held in 719 distinct LFS objects (65,110,465 bytes). The 16 patterns are `**/__Snapshots__/**/*` and `Docs/Stage0/evidence/**/*` for png, jpg, jpeg, gif, heic, tif, tiff and webp, each extension matched in any letter case, each `filter=lfs diff=lfs merge=lfs -text`. The configuration is repository-local (`git lfs install --local`; nothing in your global or the system configuration).
